5. **C199.6, C200.2: legal texts that cannot be shown as written.** A placeholder outside the key's closed list, a placeholder with no value (`organization-display-name` absent) or a stray brace makes a **legal** key `Unsupported` and a non-legal key fall back to the SDK's own words. The fill is one pass and never re-reads a filled value.
6. **C196.3: the locale-format scan** refuses `String.format` and `"…".format` unless the first argument is `Locale.ROOT`, `US` or `ENGLISH`, and any `DateTimeFormatter`, `NumberFormat`, `DecimalFormat`, `SimpleDateFormat` or `java.util.Formatter` line without such a locale or a fixed `ISO_` formatter (the last three beyond the plan's list); no hit today.
7. **C200.2:** `Sha256` is `commonMain` `flow/logic/Sha256.kt`.
8. **C196.2, C196.4: the pathological budget** is a VM step count, 4 × (input length + 1) × (program length + 1), not a CPU-time guard; inputs are capped by UTF-16 length and matched by code point, as ECMAScript does.

### android-i18n

1. **C199.1, what the key map holds.** 19 entries: the legal consent and notice texts with their screen's title, continue and saving words, and the five contest words (below). The 198 other hand-written strings whose English equals a web text are not mapped (that needs edits of the shared `values/` and `values-es/` files); their `fr`, `de` and `ar` texts were drafted from the web's own words. A later Stage 1a may map them mechanically.
2. **C199.1, C197.3: the contest words are key-mapped** (`openverify_contest_{heading,intro,host,link,contact}` → `contest.*`, legal `decision`, `flowKey` null), so the failure screen's contest route shows the web's words, never drafted ones.
3. **Checkpoint order:** each of `values-fr`, `values-de` and `values-ar` landed whole in one commit (`6a0aea9a`), its hand-written files and its catalog together, so no folder ever recorded French words under `openverify_locale` `en`.
4. **Generator provenance:** the catalogs were first written by a byte-for-byte prototype of the Gradle script; the task, once registered, rewrote them with no change, twice.
5. **C199.8, plural completeness** mirrors Phase 9's web test: `es` and `fr` may omit `many` only where a plural's documented count range cannot reach it (ranges noted per plural); any other plural needs `many`.
6. **C199.5, the resource half:** no text carries a bidi control, isolate or mark (U+200E, U+200F, U+061C, U+202A–U+202E, U+2066–U+2069) or a direction arrow (U+2190–U+21FF, U+27F5–U+27FF).
7. **C199.1, review status:** no review is recorded, so `review-status.json` carries no digests; the per-key digests are defined (`LocaleReviewStatus.keyDigests`) and the downgrade test runs them over synthetic records.
8. Existing tests changed (rule 12), no assertion weakened: `ResourcesTest` covers five locales and counts `{{name}}` placeholders; `KeyMapCatalogTest` checks the web's words in five locales and the key-map order.
9. **Ownership:** android-i18n created all seven hand-written files in each new folder (the plan named only `strings.xml`), since C199.1 needs every SDK string in all five folders. C199.4's "both paths agree" test lives in android-theme's `SdkStringTablesTest`, not in `ResourcesTest`.

### api-android-10a

1. **C196.4:** the pattern export reads Phase 9's golden cases from the data file `packages/flow-spec/src/v2/pattern/vectors.json` (not a test file); each verdict is `matchPattern`'s own and is checked against the golden one. The visibility and digits cases are copied as data into the export test; two malformed Phase 9 rules (`[true]`, `'plain'`) are left out because a refusal's rule must be an object.
2. **C199.7:** `copy-keys.json` is written by `packages/flow-spec/src/export-vectors.test.ts`. **C198.2:** `builtin-palettes.json` is read from `packages/ui/src/styles.css` (no new dependency); core's contrast test holds `BUILTIN_PALETTES` to the same stylesheet.
3. **C221.13 (Phase 7 C96.1 amended):** the native handoff skip runs where a session is answered: `skipHandoffForNativeSession` in `flow-engine/engine.ts`, called at the end of `prepareFlowSessionInTx` in `flow-engine/session.ts` (a borrowed edit beyond `engine.ts`), so before every session read and screen-submission answer, not at step entry. It skips for a session with `mobile-sdk-name` only, leaving the step through `next` with outcome `skipped`; a phone browser keeps Phase 7's view-only skip.
4. **C200.2, C221.12:** `organization-display-name` is served to every client, NFC-normalised, `null` while unset; the save refusal is the existing 422 with the field error at `/organization-display-name` (no new problem code), and also covers a name whose NFC form exceeds 100 characters; the rule is `apps/api/src/services/nfc-boundary.ts`. Not changed: `handoff-context`'s `organization-display-name` is served as stored.
5. **C200.3:** the Android consent texts are the key map's entries with a `flowKey` and legal `consent` or `notice`: 8 keys (`consent.checkbox` included), 40 rows under `android-0.1.0-75ddea08ad090412`. The catalog-version digest is the web's form (`<locale>\t<key>\t<sha256>\n`, locales `en, es, fr, de, ar`, keys sorted). The append-only guard is Phase 9's `locale-export.test.ts` git-history test.
6. **C200.4:** the three organisation digest cases are vacuous today, because no built-in consent text holds `{{organization}}` (10A decision 6), and the digest schema cannot hold custom-copy cases. Decided by integration-10a: carried to 10B Stage 1a (see the Stage 4 amendments, item 9).
7. **C200.1:** the SDK needed its catalog version at run time; android-sdk-api computes it (Stage 3 amendments, android-sdk-api item 6), so no generated constant was added.
8. **Checkpoint order:** the Testcontainers test of the digest check accepting an Android row moved from checkpoint 4 to 5, after android-i18n's last checkpoint (the rows are append-only).
9. **C224.4:** a `.multipart.json` request fixture (10B on) is parsed as the whole JSON value its owner commits; contracts-10b defines its shape. **C193.9:** no `language` query (10A decision 5).

## Workflow 10A Stage 3 amendments

Recorded by `integration-10a` on 2026-11-09 from the Stage 3 agents' reports, and from `carry-10a`'s, which ran beside Stages 1b to 3 (10A decisions item 3). Binding on 10B–10E.

### android-driver

1. **C193.3, a failed inquiry's end.** `FakeFlowServer` ends a failed inquiry's flow on the step that failed, as Phase 9's engine does (`leaveIfDecided`): a migrated v1 flow reaches its failure step only after a `next` exit. The driver shows `Failed(null, FAILED)` (C197.3's "today's failure screen"); a decided inquiry shows `Completed(<the step it names, or null>, status)`. The coordinator assertions that expected `Failed(failureStep)`/`Completed(completionStep)` changed accordingly.
2. **C193.3, the read after consent.** The consent route answers only `{consent}`, so the driver reads the session after consent; call lists that include it changed (`FlowCoordinatorTest`'s happy path and capture/retake). In the signals suites the read after consent is now the driver's own and the tiers come from it (three tests renamed or reworded, each recorded in the TDD file). `sendSignalsBeside` still runs beside the flow (rule 22).
3. **C193.4 (V02, K7A-6).** `Resolution.Finalize` is gone: a used-up or never-retryable step waits for the position to move (`Resolution.Await`, showing `Processing`), not for an inquiry failure. `FlowState.Finalizing` is no longer produced; its dispatch line, screen and outcome mapping stay until a later refactor (carried, Stage 4 item 10).
4. **The signals body's screen** on a consented load is the step the position names, not the list's first step; `FlowCoordinatorSignalsTest`'s load and K7A-7 cases and `OpenVerifyInquiryActivitySignalsTest` changed accordingly.
5. **Borrowed edits forced by the driver** (fixture lines only): `ui/ConsentLocaleTest.kt`, `OpenVerifyInquiryActivityTest.kt`, `StateHooksTest`'s android-sdk-api case, `model/Phase9WireModelTest.kt` (its skeleton assertion expects the rendered `Screen`), and `i18n/SdkStringTables.kt` regenerated by its own update mode.
6. **A hook in an unowned file:** `ui/FlowActions.kt` gained `fun submitScreen(values, button) {}` with a default body, which the view model overrides.
7. **`FlowApi.extendSelfieCapture` returns `FlowSelfieCaptureExtension(id, expiresAt)`** (`api/SelfieCaptureExtension.kt`), not `FlowSelfieCapture`: the route answers the `selfie` resource, which has no nonce and no guidance. Internal; no ABI change. `ContractFixturesTest` keeps `api/selfie.json` server-only, and `FlowApiClientAssetsTest` decodes it through the client (decided by integration-10a: no second entry, so the list's rule "a decoder or server-only, never both" holds).
8. **C195.9:** android-screens' `screenUnsupportedReason(step)` is the one judge of a screen's limits, unknown kinds and refused visibility rules; the driver's duplicate `ScreenLimits` was removed before GREEN.
9. **C199.7 at the driver:** `ServerPosition.render` refuses a step whose operator copy holds a legal key the SDK cannot place (`Unsupported("copy <key>")`) and logs `flow.copy_key_ignored` for an ignored one, on each render (not once per session; for `privacy-checks` in 10C to judge).
10. `FlowHandoff.done` is read only by `ContractFixturesTest`; the session's `handoff` member is recorded `ignored` in `flow-spec-members.json` (Stage 1a item 9).
11. **C219.6:** 78 test invocations removed (FlowNavigatorTest 9, JsonLogicTest 36, FlowCoordinatorProjectionTest 26, FlowCoordinatorHandoffTest's done-handoff case 3, FlowCoordinatorTest 3, FlowSpecHeaderTest's "no production path calls them" scan 1), names in `docs/tdd/phase10-android-driver.tdd.md`.
12. **C201.5 (`keepScreenOn` on the waiting screen)** is 10B's (capture-pipeline), as a borrowed edit of `ui/StatusScreens.kt`.
13. **C199.1 finding (carried to 10B Stage 1a):** the web catalogs hold `screen.waiting.title`, `screen.waiting.message`, `screen.halted.title` and `screen.halted.message`; the driver's `openverify_waiting_*` and `openverify_halted_*` mean the same and should be key-mapped (moved into `strings_catalog.xml` and added to `SDK_COPY_KEYS`). C197.2's halted wording ("the organisation can resume it") differs from the web's ("contact the organisation"); the owner picks one.

### android-screens

1. **The screens' environment** (`LocalScreenEnvironment`) had no provider: in the app every `screen` step rendered unsupported, verification steps got no prefill and the failure screen no contest route. integration-10a provides it (Stage 4 item 3).
2. **C194.1, C195: a screen the renderer cannot render** for a component text no catalog holds shows the unsupported screen inside `FlowState.Screen`, so closing it reports `Canceled`, not `Failed(UNSUPPORTED_FLOW)`. Carried to 10B Stage 1a: the registry or `ServerPosition` should also call `screenTextsComplete(step, screenEnvironmentOf(session, language).text)` and render `FlowState.Unsupported("screen copy")`.
3. **C195.2 amended (review P07).** Secrets (`masked_text`, the OTP code, a document number; in 10C the MRZ and the CAN) use a plain text or number keyboard with autocorrect off, not a password keyboard: Compose 1.11's `CoreTextFieldSemanticsModifierNode` sets `ContentType.Password` for `KeyboardType.Password` and `NumberPassword`, which would declare the field a password to every autofill service. Compose 1.11 cannot set `IME_FLAG_NO_PERSONALIZED_LEARNING` or mark one node not important for autofill, so a keyboard may still learn what is typed into a secret field; the TDD file records it for 10D's review.
4. **Phase 5 behaviour changed:** the OTP code drops `ContentType.SmsOtpCode` and the `NumberPassword` keyboard (Number keyboard, no content type), so SMS code autofill is no longer offered; no existing test asserted it.
5. **C213.1 per API level:** Robolectric's offline jar is API 36 only and rule 9 forbids fetching others, so API 26 to 35 are covered through the injected `LocalPlatformApiLevel` and pure tables; data sensitivity is Compose's `isSensitiveData`, and the obscured-touch filter is `dropObscuredTouches()` (no per-node `filterTouchesWhenObscured` in Compose). The protections also cover the document step's submit, both wallet submits and the screen's cancel confirmation.
6. New platform files: `ui/screen/Protections.{android,ios}.kt` (the actuals of `platformApiLevel()` and `Modifier.dropObscuredTouches()`; the iOS one claims nothing).
7. **C199.1:** the screen's 36 strings copy the web's `screen.*` words, hand-written in `strings_screen.xml` (no legal text among them); key-mapping them is optional for a later Stage 1a.
8. **C196.3:** the device-locale test runs each of the five locales twice; only `ar-EG` can share its language with the SDK's choice, so the others run with two different bundled languages.
9. **C195.1, C195.2 choices:** spacer sizes 8, 16 and 32 dp; the stepper a vertical list stating the current step; the phone component a country selector and a number field, sent in international form, checked for shape only (`+` and 7 to 15 digits; 7 provisional, ours; 15 from E.164); the date a typed ISO date plus Material's date picker, whose month names follow the device's language and whose window inherits `FLAG_SECURE`; the cancel confirmation shown in place.

### android-theme

1. **The `errorContainer` defect** (HIGH): fixed by integration-10a (Stage 4 item 2).
2. **Hooks:** the session's look reaches `OpenVerifyTheme` through android-sdk-api's `InquiryLaunchScope` (Stage 3, android-sdk-api item 9), not through new coordinator members; the hooks this report proposed in `FlowCoordinator.kt` and `InquiryViewModel.kt` are not needed.
3. **C199.4 as built:** the generated tables live in `commonMain/.../i18n/SdkStringTables.kt` (android-theme's), generated and guarded by `SdkStringTablesTest`. **Whoever adds or changes an SDK string regenerates them in the same commit** (`UPDATE_SDK_STRING_TABLES=1 … --tests dev.openverify.sdk.i18n.SdkStringTablesTest --rerun`). Proposed for 10B Stage 1a: a `generateSdkStringTables` task the compilation depends on (a shared build-file change). The "both paths agree" assertion is in `SdkStringTablesTest`, over every string raw and filled and every plural at 18 counts, in all five locales.
4. **C199.5, display isolation:** `sdkString` keeps the logical text (consent digests unchanged); `sdkText`/`sdkPluralText` give the displayed text with U+2068/U+2069 around each argument. Call sites in other owners' files that show a value inside a sentence still use `sdkString`; each owner, or a 10D fixer, moves them.
5. **C198.3 amended:** the server's rendition emits PNG and JPEG (`apps/api/src/services/themes/assets.ts:4-9`), so `checkLogo` accepts both by their magic bytes and refuses every other format; the header bounds are read by a pure `commonMain` parser (PNG IHDR; JPEG's first SOFn), so the check runs on iOS too.
6. **C198.10:** Phase 9 defines one placement, `header`; any other value takes the header. **C198.9 and C194.2's style-override row:** dormant (K9-4); no override path or golden.
7. **C197.4, C199.3:** the help slot honours the operator's `screen.helpLink` and `screen.privacyLink` without a key-map entry (proposed for 10B Stage 1a: map them, legal null). The selector is hidden when fewer than two enabled languages are bundled, offered on every screen, and a choice after consent is not reported (10A decision 5).
8. **Logo alt text** resolves like a component text, falling back to the organisation's name, then to `openverify_logo_description`.
9. Existing tests changed: `StateHooksTest`'s android-theme case replaced by its owner; `StepScaffoldTest`'s "never mirrored" case draws the window into a bitmap (assertion unchanged).

### android-sdk-api

1. **C190.2, where the loopback rule applies.** `Request` and `OneTimeLink` are built before any activity, so their constructors keep `ApiBaseUrl`'s grammar (https, or http to `localhost`, `127.0.0.1`, `::1`, `10.0.2.2`); the activity reads `LoopbackHttp.read(applicationContext)` on each `onCreate`, and a launch for which `LoopbackPolicy.allows(apiBaseUrl)` is false ends `Failed(INVALID_REQUEST)` before any request. A request for the SDK's own `stackTest` goes through `FlowApiClient`'s grammar directly.
2. **C190.2:** the "two links on different origins reach the same API" tests passed at once (Stage 1a's public signature already ignored the origin); the internal one-argument reader is deleted.
3. **Existing tests changed (rule 12):** `OneTimeLinksTest` and `OneTimeLinksParityTest` read every link with the host's API; `OpenVerifyInquiryOneTimeLinkTest` rewritten on the required API; the activity tests set `LoopbackHttp.testOverride`; `ConsentScreenTest`'s optional text expects the days isolated in the displayed text; `StateHooksTest`'s android-sdk-api case replaced; `ConsentEvidenceBodyTest` uses `consentCatalogVersion()`; `NeverReadTest` (named borrowed edit) lists `HIDE_OVERLAY_WINDOWS` and the `LoopbackPolicy` actual among the `FLAG_DEBUGGABLE` readers.
4. **C213.1:** `OverlayWindows.apply` calls `setHideOverlayWindows` from API 31 only; a `SecurityException` is logged as `activity.overlay_hiding_refused` and never stops the flow.
5. **C200.5, a missing hook carried to 10B Stage 1a.** A 422 `consent_text_mismatch` is turned into a conflict by `events/ObservedFlowApi` (activity path only) and logged `consent.text_mismatch`; the person sees the generic "out of sync" retry, whose retry reads the session and shows the consent again; no consent is recorded silently. The exact hook: `FlowState.Consent.textChanged`, set by `FlowCoordinator.sendConsent` on that problem after `adopt(api.getSession())`, with a notice in `ConsentScreen` (new strings in five folders), then the mapping removed. The C200.5 "PII-free event" is the log code: the public event set has no kind for it.
6. **C200.2, a missing hook carried to 10B Stage 1a.** An operator legal consent text that cannot be shown as written shows the unsupported screen inside the consent screen, so closing reports `Canceled`, not `Failed(UNSUPPORTED_FLOW)`. The exact hook: `consentState(step)` returns `unsupported("consent text <key>")` when a required key's text resolves `ConsentText.Unavailable` with `ConsentValues.of(session)` in any language the session's copy carries or among the SDK's texts.
7. **C200.1, `copy-source`:** `custom` when every text is the operator's, `built-in` when none is, left out when mixed: the server matches a stated source against that source only. The web flow sent `custom` whenever any text was custom, which the server refuses; fixed test-first by integration-10a (Stage 4 item 5).
8. **C200.1, the catalog version** is computed once at run time from the SDK's generated tables with `exportConsentTexts`'s algorithm (`consentCatalogVersion()` = `android-0.1.0-75ddea08ad090412`), held to the 40 rows by `ConsentTextTest`; a text missing from a locale's table changes the version, so the server refuses the consent. No device normaliser: built-in texts are NFC at build time, copy and names arrive NFC, and a filled form that is not NFC is refused by the server (fail closed).
9. **C198, C199: the activity applies the session's look.** `OpenVerifyTheme(look = SessionLook.of(<last session>), appearance, requestedLocale, language, themeAssets)` from `InquiryLaunchScope`, which sees each session through `ObservedFlowApi` (a `FlowApi` decorator in `events/FlowEvents.kt`); `LanguageSelection` lives in that scope, so a rotation keeps the person's choice.
10. **C192.2, how each event is detected:** `Submitted` follows an accepted consent, verification submission, wallet signature or screen submission (not OTP confirmation, inputs or uploads); `StepShown` on a new step id; `Waiting` once per stretch of Waiting, Processing or Finalizing; no event for a refused launch; events exist on the activity path only; `installed()` is read once per launch.
11. **Missing hooks carried to 10B (capture-pipeline):** `CameraPermission(granted = false)` (the denial in `CameraPermission.android.kt`) and `CaptureFailed(CAMERA_UNAVAILABLE)` (the binding failure in `CameraXCaptureCamera.Preview`).
12. **C191.3:** 10A tests the absent listener and the absent transport; the chip reader, face detector and integrity provider are read from 10B and 10C.
13. Borrowed edits: `NeverReadTest.kt` (`886656a2`), `AndroidDeviceSignals.kt` (`bf50a0ff`, `03f2ab83`), `FlowCoordinator.sendSignalsBeside` (three lines, `03f2ab83`), `android/openverify-sdk/README.md` (`c175bcba`, `12008fa9`).

### carry-10a (beside Stages 1b to 3)

1. **KP10-1 (C171.3):** a race in the check, not a real cover: `coveredFocus` (`e2e/tests/support/ui-a11y.ts`) judges the settled page, two animation frames after the key press, then each frame for up to 1,000 ms (provisional, ours); a lasting cover still fails. `toasts.tsx` is unchanged. The e2e job's upload sets `include-hidden-files: true`; the 10A push's artifact confirms or refutes that this was why no trace was uploaded.
2. **KP10-2:** the gitleaks installer has its own bounded retry (5 attempts, a doubling 2 s delay, `--connect-timeout 20 --max-time 60`) on a 5xx, a timeout or a connection failure, failing at once on any 4xx (`--retry-all-errors` would retry 4xx); `GITLEAKS_CACHE_DIR` and `GITLEAKS_RETRY_DELAY_SECONDS` are new optional settings; the infra and secrets jobs cache the verified release, keyed on the installer file.
3. **KP10-3:** the judged frame's full-resolution copy travels in `SampledFrame.held`, with `FlowEnvironment.sampleFrame` still declaring `RgbaFrame | null` (tightening its type is optional); `onAutoCapture(state, held)`. The rule for 10B's port (C204): an auto-capture uploads the judged frame's copy, the manual shutter the live frame, and nothing is kept after the upload.
4. **K9-7:** the draft page reads `GET /workflows?filter[kind]=module` (at most 200, provisional) with no route-schema change; `page.test.tsx` serves an empty list. Finding carried: `palette-model.ts`'s `paletteOf` should refuse module entries when the trigger becomes `trigger-account` or `trigger-case` after the page loaded (`module_refused_for_trigger`).
5. **R002 (C160.6):** `readFlowThemeAsset` takes `{environmentId, inquiryId}` and serves only the logo and uploaded font of the session's resolved theme; one existing test opens its session on an inquiry whose theme set names the font it reads (assertions unchanged).
6. **R004 (C161.5):** `LocaleReviewStatus`'s reviewed and partially-reviewed variants gain an optional `keySha256`; `servedReviewStatus` treats it as the snapshot only when its catalog digest matches, else serves every key unreviewed (fail closed), so a future reviewed record must carry `keySha256`. `apps/flow/src/i18n/locale-export.ts` still writes the declared status; when a review is recorded, its owner writes `servedReviewStatus` there.
7. **R008:** the refresh touches inquiries, verifications, checks and cases; the benchmark seed holds no cases (Phase 11's follow-up).
8. Rule 24 as run: the one-call own-specs form ran out at load 33 to 42, so the specs ran in rule 7's separate calls on one fresh stack.

## Workflow 10A Stage 4 amendments (integration-10a)

Recorded by `integration-10a` on 2026-11-09 (evidence in `docs/tdd/phase10-integration-10a.tdd.md`). Binding on 10B–10E.

1. **No public, route or fixture change after Stage 1.** No Stage 2–3 report requested a route-schema or public-signature change, so `docs/api/openapi.json` (sha256 `4b53b129…cf52`, `openapi:write` gives no diff), `@openverify/node`, `contracts/fixtures/api/` (194 files) and the ABI dumps with `expected-public.txt` are unchanged since Stages 1a and 1b; the consent texts did not change after api-android-10a, so neither export was re-run.
2. **C198.2 amended (android-theme's HIGH finding):** `errorContainer` takes **`canvas`** under `danger`, not `surface`. Material 3's `contentColorFor` picks the first role equal to a container's colour, and it checks `errorContainer` before `surface`, so with `errorContainer = surface` every `Surface` drew its body text in `danger`. Rule: no role `contentColorFor` checks before `surface` may equal `surface`; `ThemeMappingTest.textOnTheSurfaceFamilyIsInkNotDanger` holds it over the built-in palettes and an operator palette. 21 goldens were recorded again by the explicit record call (15 android-screens, 6 android-theme).
3. **The screens' environment (android-screens' missing hook).** `OpenVerifyInquiryActivity` provides `LocalScreenEnvironment` = `screenEnvironmentOf(<last session>, LocalSdkLanguage, drafts = InquiryViewModel.screenDrafts)`; `InquiryViewModel.screenDrafts` (memory only) is cleared with the other drafts when the flow leaves the step. The asset loader is `{ null }`: a `screen`'s `image` shows its text alternative until a decoded loader with C198.3's checks exists (carried to 10B Stage 1a).
4. **The unsupported screen's reason (android-driver's missing hook):** the dispatcher passes `FlowState.Unsupported.reason`, so a server before flow-spec 2 is said to be older than the app.
5. **C162.9, a defect in Phase 9's web flow (android-sdk-api's finding):** the web consent sent `copy-source: custom` whenever any text was the operator's, which the server's check refuses for a partly custom consent. It now says `custom` only when every text shown was the operator's, `built-in` when none was, and leaves it out when they are mixed, as Android does. Existing test changed (rule 12): `consent-step.test.tsx`'s "says the words were the operator copy when the session replaced one" became the mixed case, and a wholly custom case was added.
6. **C224.3 as built.** The 10A flow is consent, a sandbox `database` step (a version needs a verification step, and the sandbox database decides it with no camera), a content screen, a branch on `session.locale` (route `spanish`, `onNull: else`), a Spanish screen and a completion; it is the committed `e2e/fixtures/android-10a/flow.json`, held to its builder `e2e/src/android-flow.ts` by an assert-only test, so both clients drive the same flow. `e2e/tests/flow-android.spec.ts` (2 tests, ≤ 90 s each, about 1.2 s each on the stack) is the API-driven client with the Android `User-Agent` and `OpenVerify-Flow-Spec: 2`; D2 is shown by the branch's exit port in the step record. It joined chromium shard 1.
7. **C224.4 as built.** `AndroidFlowStackTest` is a Robolectric test (JUnit 4, `@Category(StackTests::class)`), so the real `AndroidDeviceSignals` runs with an application context; it builds `FlowApiClient` directly (its own URL grammar allows http to `127.0.0.1`), so `LoopbackHttp.testOverride` is not needed. The `stackTest` task passes `openverify.e2e.stackRecord` and `openverify.e2e.androidFlow`, never counts as up to date, and no longer passes with no test. It asserts the signal body was stored: the inquiry session's `signals-collected` holds `session-integrity`, its `device-type` is set (only an SDK body sets it), and `inquiry/bot_score` is 95.
8. **C220.1, C219.6 as built.** The `android` job runs, after its first Gradle step, `./gradlew :openverify-sdk:screenshotTest -Proborazzi.test.verify=true checkKotlinAbi :demo-app:bundleRelease` and uploads `build/outputs/roborazzi/` with the reports; no CI step records a golden. The SDK host-test floor is **1,934**: 1,366 − 78 (android-driver's deletions) = 1,288, + 646 added in 10A. `infra/tests/ci-workflow.test.sh` holds both (264 checks). The push is the CI proof of the goldens on a GitHub runner: Robolectric's native graphics should draw the same pixels on any Linux x86_64 host, but a font-rendering difference there would fail the job, and its fix is test-first in 10B's Stage 1a, never a raised threshold.
9. **Carried to 10B Stage 1a, each with its exact change in the Stage 3 amendments:** the consent-mismatch screen hook (android-sdk-api 5); a consent legal text that cannot be filled, and a screen whose component text no catalog holds, ending `Failed(UNSUPPORTED_FLOW)` instead of `Canceled` (android-sdk-api 6, android-screens 2); the custom-copy digest cases in `android-consent-digests.json`'s schema (api-android-10a 6: `{copy-source: custom, text, values, text-sha256}`, asserted through the real consent screen); key-mapping the driver's waiting and halted strings and the help-slot links (android-driver 13, android-theme 7); a `generateSdkStringTables` task (android-theme 3); the decoded theme-asset loader for `image` components (item 3); `CameraPermission(false)` and `CaptureFailed(CAMERA_UNAVAILABLE)` (android-sdk-api 11); `keepScreenOn` on the waiting screen (android-driver 12); making `FileKind`'s and `InquiryStatus`'s serializers internal (Stage 1b amendment 3). To 10D: `FlowState.Finalizing`'s dead dispatch, screen and mapping (android-driver 3); the display call sites still on `sdkString` (android-theme 4); `flow.copy_key_ignored` once per session (android-driver 9). To the console's owner: `palette-model.ts`'s module refusal for a trigger changed after load (carry-10a 4).
10. **D4 (C200.6, C221.9).** No console view or API shows a consent record's `client` or digests, so their "client-reported" label is carried until one exists; `docs/android/themes-and-languages.md` states what the digests prove and do not. The preview's list of differences gained the Android line (`lib/copy/preview.ts`, `preview-banner.test.tsx`).
11. **Records.** `docs/android/` (README, integration, migration, events, failure reasons, themes and languages; 10B and 10C add the rest of C222's list), `docs/PLAN.md` (§3 zxing-cpp; §6 the five Workflows, the optional artifacts and P1–P5 as decided; §9 two risk rows), `docs/research/persona-surface.md`'s Android rows, `docs/checks/sandbox-providers.md`'s Android control (named borrowed edit).
12. **Process.** The CI commit uses the type `ci:`, which is not in `CLAUDE.md`'s list (as foundation-10a's `build(sdk)`); nothing was amended.

What api-integrity builds: `inquiry/bot_score` reads `android-integrity` through its integrity group, `android_debugger_connected` as medium evidence (`medium-evidence-min`) and `android_app_debuggable` as weak (`weak-evidence-max`), never strong, so P24's cap holds and neither alone reaches the strong band; a body with neither true reads the low band with `no_artefact_found` and its capped confidence, never "human"; `automation_artefact_count` counts `android_debugger_connected` on an Android session (0 or 1); `behavior_threat_level` stays null with `input_missing`. Each such entry's version moves with its inputs (api-integrity's commit; this Stage did not change those entries).
18. **C211.3, C211.4.** The integrity schemas, `DEVICE_INTEGRITY_PROVIDERS` (`play-integrity`, `sandbox`) and `SANDBOX_INTEGRITY_TOKENS` live in `packages/contracts/src/flow-integrity.ts`; the provider kind is named `deviceIntegrity` in `apps/api/src/providers/index.ts`'s camelCase table (registration: foundation-10b). The session's `integrity` member is **not appearance-only** (honoured in `flow-spec-members.json`).
19. **Not done in this Stage, handed on.** `DeviceSignals.collect` stays non-suspend: it is internal and lives in `signals/`, device-integrity-android's path, which converts it with its own tests (the call site in `sendSignalsBeside` already runs in a coroutine, so no hook change). The legal texts of decision 66 (`nativeApp.notice.session-integrity`'s probe-naming text, `notice.play-integrity` in `COPY_KEYS`, the five web catalogs, the key map, `SDK_COPY_KEYS`, `ConsentTexts` and the consent re-exports) are **not done**: see the report's blockers; integration-10b or the orchestrator schedules them before api-integrity's `play_integrity_notice_missing` rule needs the key.

## Workflow 10B Stage 1b amendments (foundation-10b)

Recorded by `foundation-10b` on 2026-11-09 (its commits from `6cfc8ab1`; checkpoints `29ecca49` (1, the SDK hooks), `1aa0f4bc` (2, (C) server), `e1534863` (3, (I) server), and the final gates; evidence in `docs/tdd/phase10-foundation-10b.tdd.md`). Binding on 10B's Stage 2 and 3 agents; each names the contract it amends or settles.

1. **C206, the SDK hooks as built.** The capture screen takes every still through one action, `takeStill(camera, trigger, onCaptured): Boolean` (`ui/CaptureScreen.kt`), which the shutter runs with `captureTriggerOf(auto = false)` and the auto-capture (`screenAutoCapture`) with the trigger it is given; `onCaptured` and `FlowActions.capture` gained `trigger: CaptureTrigger?` (default null). The coordinator keeps each kept photo's `Shot` (picked, camera facts, time, trigger) per kind and passes `captureMethodOf(step, picked)` and the trigger to `uploadFile` (an ID side, a pose, a document's camera photo) and `captureMethodOf(step, picked = true)` to `uploadDocument`. Both deciders (`camera/CaptureTrigger.kt`) and `screenAutoCapture` (`capture/AutoCapture.kt`) are `capture-pipeline`'s stubs answering nothing, so every upload is today's until it decides them.
2. **C204.5, the extension's shape.** `SelfieCaptureWindow(id, expiresAt: ComparableTimeMark)` lives in `capture/selfie/Extension.kt` (`selfie-guidance`'s, so its owner may add members with defaults); the coordinator publishes `selfieCapture: StateFlow<SelfieCaptureWindow?>` (the open capture's id and its expiry on `Settings.timeSource`: creation plus `CAPTURE_TTL`), and `extendSelfieCapture()` calls `extendCapture(api, window)` through its constructor parameter `selfieExtension` and adopts the answered `expiresAt` only while the same capture is open; `ActiveCapture.expired()` reads that expiry, so a pose after the old expiry goes to the extended capture. The dispatch (`InquiryFlowScreen`) draws `SelfieCaptureExtension(window, actions::extendSelfieCapture)` over a selfie's capture screen in a `Box`; `FlowActions.selfieCapture` and `extendSelfieCapture()` have defaults for test doubles. Open for `selfie-guidance` and the server: a pose's `client-elapsed-ms` is still counted from the capture's creation and clamped at 600,000 (`SelfieCaptureUpload.MAX_CLIENT_ELAPSED_MS`), which ten extensions can pass.
3. **C211.3, the SDK's integrity hook.** `signals/Integrity.kt` (`device-integrity-android`'s) declares `fun interface IntegrityVerdictRequest { suspend fun requestIntegrityVerdict(session: FlowSession) }` with `None`, and `integrityVerdictRequest(api, provider): IntegrityVerdictRequest` (stub: `None`); each launch installs it over the flow's client and `OpenVerifyComponents.integrityTokenProvider` (`InquiryViewModelFactory.kt`). The coordinator runs it on a consented load (inside the `sendSignalsBeside` guard) and after consent, with the session read, as a child of the calling action, never awaited, failures swallowed; whether the session asks for a token (`integrity.request`) and "one per inquiry" are the request's to decide, so the coordinator calls it on each such read.
4. **C201.5 (KP10-13).** `ui/KeepScreenOn.kt` `@Composable internal expect fun KeepScreenOn()`, Android actual through `LocalView` with a per-view holder count, iOS actual nothing; `WaitingScreen` calls it. `capture-pipeline` calls the same composable from the capture screens, `nfc-sdk` (10C) from the tap screen.
5. **C219.2, C219.3.** `GoldenOwner` gains `CAPTURE_PIPELINE`, `DOCUMENT_GUIDANCE`, `PDF417_GUIDANCE` and `SELFIE_GUIDANCE` with their folders. Kover files for the glue contracts-10b named: `kover-excludes/capture-pipeline.txt` (`dev.openverify.sdk.camera.CameraXFrameAnalyzer*`) and `kover-excludes/pdf417-guidance.txt` (`dev.openverify.sdk.capture.barcode.ZxingCppBarcodeReader*`); each class name is proposed, and its owner keeps or corrects it in its own file.
6. **C221.3, a column the plan's list lacks.** `inquiry_integrity_challenges` gains `token` (sealed for the challenge's row, nullable) and `created_at`. The `integrity-decode` job carries ids only (C211.4; rule 11 keeps a token out of Valkey), so the token the device posts must wait somewhere for the job: sealed in its challenge's row from the post until the decode reads and nulls it (`takeInquiryIntegrityToken`), with a CHECK that only a used challenge holds one. `ENCRYPTED_COLUMNS` therefore lists both `inquiry_integrity_challenges.token` and `inquiry_integrity_verdicts.verdict` (`aad: 'row'`), and `REWRAP_TABLES` both tables (`ID_COLUMNS.inquiry_integrity_verdicts = 'inquiry_id'`). The verdicts carry `created_at` and `updated_at` with the `set_updated_at` trigger.
7. **C221.3, deletion.** The inquiry's redaction deletes both tables' rows (`deleteInquiryIntegrityRecordsInTx`). The C16 pass deletes a challenge `INTEGRITY_CHALLENGE_RETENTION_HOURS` (24, provisional) past its expiry, with any token still in it (`deleteExpiredIntegrityChallenges`), and any integrity record a redacted inquiry still holds (`deleteIntegrityRecordsOfRedactedInquiries`); `RetentionReport` gains `deletedIntegrityChallenges` and `cleanedIntegrityRecordInquiries`. A verdict otherwise lives as long as its inquiry.
8. **C211.4, the provider and the job.** The provider stubs live in `apps/api/src/services/device-integrity/` and `providers/index.ts` imports `createDeviceIntegrityProvider` from there (the plan names that directory; the other kinds' providers live in `providers/`). The port is `DeviceIntegrityProvider { name; decode({ token, requestHash }): Promise<DeviceIntegrityVerdict> }` (`ports.ts`), the verdict typed as an opaque record `api-integrity` may narrow. The `play-integrity` binding needs settings (the package name, the certificate digest, the service account key) that no configuration reads yet: `api-integrity` adds them to `config.ts` with their environment variables, stored as the existing provider secrets are.
9. **C211.3, the session member.** `getFlowSession` asks `integritySessionMember(deps, { environmentId, environmentKind, inquiryId, sessionId, signals })` and leaves the member out while it answers `null` (the stub), so no session changes until `api-integrity` builds it; `{ request: false }` and an absent member both mean no request to the SDK.
10. **C221.7.** `flow-integrity` is counted by the flow scope's `preParsing` hook (`plugins/rate-limit.ts`, `FLOW_INTEGRITY_ROUTES`) per inquiry for both routes together, beside `flow-session`, as `flow-signals` and `flow-handoffs` are; the routes enforce nothing themselves. Default 5, window 3600 s, console label "Device integrity, per inquiry".
11. **The frozen routes** declare 501 among their problems until `api-integrity` builds them (as 9B's frozen routes did; `phase9b-openapi-problems.test.ts`'s pattern), and `docs/api/openapi.json` was written with them (sha256 `f4e3f754…c2c0`); integration-10b regenerates it after the build drops 501. `INTEGRITY_TOKEN_MAX_BODY_BYTES` (16 KiB + 1,024) caps the token route's body (413).
12. **Process.** The shared tree held the Stage 2 agents' uncommitted and RED work throughout, so a whole-suite Android run here failed only in their paths (the TDD file names each); every suite over this Stage's files passed. `packages/db`'s tests resolve `@openverify/core` through its `dist`, so a change to core's exports needs `pnpm --filter @openverify/core build` before the db suite (and before `drizzle-kit generate`).

## Workflow 10B Stage 2 amendments

Recorded by `integration-10b` on 2026-11-09 from the six Stage 2 reports (each agent's contractDeviations, condensed; their TDD files in `docs/tdd/phase10-<agent>.tdd.md` hold the evidence). Binding on 10C onward; where Stage 3 settled an item, the Stage 3 section below says how.

### capture-pipeline

1. **KP10-14 without editing the activity.** `CaptureCamera.reportUnavailableTo(listener)` (no-op default); `EventingCaptureCamera` registers `captureFailed(CAMERA_UNAVAILABLE)` on it and forwards `Preview(lens, analysis, modifier)` and `frames()` (borrowed edit of `events/FlowEvents.kt`); the refusal is told from `rememberCameraPermission` through `InquiryLaunchScope.of(owner)`, which reads, never creates, the launch scope. Events go out on the activity path only.
2. **C201.1.** A second internal `Preview(lens, analysis: AnalysisTarget, modifier)` (default delegates); `AnalysisTarget` DOCUMENT 640, BARCODE 1280, SELFIE 320 (4:3). **C201.2 / F11:** an ID back's analysis is a 1280x960 stream, not a full-resolution crop (CameraX guarantees an analysis stream up to the preview size beside a full-size still); the device test logs both copy costs.
3. **Rule 19 read as the pixel buffers:** one reused buffer per frame size; each sampled frame still allocates a small descriptor, a hand-off and a `CompletableDeferred`. Provisional: `FrameRelay` hand-off wait 50 ms, release wait 1 s; `FrameSampler` 8 Hz on a fixed schedule.
4. **Callbacks on the composition's dispatcher.** A guidance never buffers, conflates or `flowOn`s `frames()`; heavy measures use `withContext(Dispatchers.Default)` inside the callback.
5. **C213.1** on the shutter, "upload a photo instead" and "use this photo" only; retake, back and cancel stay unprotected. A second still during the shutter's answers `false`.
6. **Kover:** `kover-excludes/capture-pipeline.txt` excludes `dev.openverify.sdk.camera.ProcessCameraBinder*` (foundation-10b's `CameraXFrameAnalyzer*` removed: the analyzer is `FrameAnalyzer`, covered). No strings, no goldens.
7. Changed with the plan (rule 12): `StateHooksTest`'s capture-pipeline pin replaced; one `CaptureHooksTest` case expects `captureMethodOf`'s answer. **Hooks it left** (settled in Stage 3): the selfie factory in the activity; `nativeCaptureMethods` in the coordinator's offer checks.

### document-guidance

1. **C202.3, the hints:** six in a fixed order (move closer, hold still, tilt away, more light, fit the card, show the MRZ band); HOLD_STILL only when lit, glare-free and framed; brightness above the maximum maps to "tilt away"; MOVE_CLOSER means one of 9 smaller centred guides (50-90%) is framed. The MRZ hint and gate apply only to the front of a passport-only `government_id` step: such a page is never auto-taken without its band (guidance, never a result). A hint is shown and announced only after 500 ms.
2. **C202.1, the measures:** the four server measures over the guide's crop read as its own image (BORDER_REFLECT_101); the framing edge ratio is the second-weakest border's best line mean of Sobel energy within +/-6% of the shorter side over a strip beyond it plus a floor of 100 (good frames 49-827, broken 0.23-7.3); the conversions match OpenCV 5.0.0 exactly (grey `(9798R+19235G+3735B+16384)>>15`; HSV fixed point), so the vector tolerances are near zero.
3. **Scope:** government ID FRONT and BACK only; a supporting document's page keeps the manual shutter (the calibration covers ID renders only). The analysis guide is placed at 90% of the upright frame; it agrees with the preview in shape and centre, not to the pixel.
4. **C202.4 as built:** `run()` returns after a successful `takeStill`; a failed one promotes the shutter and ends auto-capture for that instance; frames completing without a capture promote the shutter; 10 s promotion by a coroutine delay; holds on `TimeSource` marks.
5. **Strings** appended to Phase 5's `strings_document.xml` (`openverify_document_guidance_*`, with `fit_page` and an auto-capture note). Two Python RED expectations were corrected before GREEN (recorded); the calibration test accepts each threshold within 1% (OpenCV SIMD paths).
6. **Hook it left** (settled in Stage 3): the preview's passport guide.

### pdf417-guidance

1. **C203.3, the calibration** runs on the host with the vision environment's zxing-cpp 3.1.1 (asserted equal to the AAR's) and the Android options: reliable from 512 px (1.62 px a module), so the symbol must span 80% of a 640 px frame or 40% of a 1280 px one; the read stays a hint and gates nothing in 10B.
2. **What marks a side barcode-bearing:** the session has no member for it, so `isBarcodeBearing(state)` reads the existing `barcode_not_found` retry reason; the barcode overlay first shows on a retry. Proposed for a later contracts agent: a session member, from the template's required barcode checks, naming the barcode-bearing side.
3. **C219.5 assets** are Java resources under `androidDeviceTest/resources/capture/barcode/` (in the test APK); full size 950 px and the 640 px analysis width; 1280 px is covered by the span model.
4. **Composition:** `BarcodeGuidanceFactory(next)` wraps the document factory; the barcode read runs first in the same callback; the document guidance alone takes the photo and owns `shutterPrimary`. Two `SdkLog` codes: `barcode.read_failed`, `barcode.reader_unavailable`. Kover keeps `ZxingCppBarcodeReader*`.
5. **Open:** `Pdf417Guidance.ANALYSIS_WIDTH_PX` and `DocumentCaptureConstants.BARCODE_ANALYSIS_WIDTH_PX` both say 1280 (carried: one constant); whether a barcode side is analysed at 1280 px is capture-pipeline's `AnalysisTarget.of(FileKind.BACK)` (every back), not `isBarcodeBearing`.

### selfie-guidance

1. **C204 and KP10-3 (10B decisions item 6):** the auto-capture calls `AutoCapture.takeStill()` from the frame callback in which the machine fires; the upload is the `ImageCapture` still, never the 320 px analysis frame.
2. **C204.1:** the plan's "score 0.9" is the machine's face threshold; the decoder keeps the web detector's candidate threshold 0.6 and NMS 0.3, pinned by vectors on each side. The Android tensor is built from the raw analysis frame turned upright and sampled to the nearest pixel at 320 px; rotation and scaling are Kotlin-only tests (the vectors cover upright frames).
3. **C204.5:** the extension's outcome (extended, failed, already sent) travels through `internal object SelfieExtensions` because `extendCapture(api, window)` returns only the window (suggested: return the outcome and drop the object); a granted extension moves the device's expiry by `CAPTURE_TTL` (180 s), the server's fixed move; a 409 `selfie_capture_not_extendable` reads the session once and reports `ALREADY_SENT` or `FAILED`, never resending; the warning is a card at the top in foundation-10b's `Box`.
4. **Rule 19:** one mutable `GuidanceMachine` per prompt (NaN and `NO_TIME` for "none"); detection, decoding and judgement run on `Dispatchers.Default` inside the callback; tensor, candidates and faces zeroed before it returns (C204.8).
5. **C205.4:** the detector is created once per prompt; `openverify-face` may cache its session inside its factory.
6. Vector machine steps carry no announcement or centre ratio member (side prompts use `constants.machine-centre-yaw-ratio`); the instance is not schema-validated in `apps/flow` (no Ajv). Borrowed edits: `review-status.json` (16 keys), `StateHooksTest`'s selfie pin. **Hooks it left** (settled in Stage 3): the window's guidance, the activity's selfie factory, `SelfieExtensions.reset()`.

### device-integrity-android

1. **C211.3 hook gap:** contracts-10b's no-argument stubs could not send; the encoders are `IntegrityRequests.challenge(http, apiBase, authorization)` and `.token(...)` (settled in Stage 3: `FlowApiClient` calls them).
2. **C210.2/C210.3 from public `Build` fields only:** `qemu_property` when board, brand, device, hardware, manufacturer, model or product contains "qemu", or brand and device both start with "generic"; `ro_debuggable_and_not_secure` when `Build.TYPE == "eng"`. `xposed_in_stack_trace` reads the main looper thread's stack (the probes run on `Dispatchers.IO`).
3. **Absent versus empty (refines Stage 1a amendment 15):** each list is present, possibly empty, whenever the probes ran; a family is left out only when every one of its reads threw.
4. **C193.5 for `integrity_challenge_invalid`:** the request reads the session once and stops, rendering nothing (it runs beside the flow). "Never before a capture screen" is read as never holding or delaying a screen. "At most once per inquiry" is enforced per launch; across launches the server's `integrity.request` governs (api-integrity answers `false` once a token was taken or a verdict exists).
5. **Rule 12 / C210.3:** `collect` suspends and reads on `Dispatchers.IO`; the tests that drove it synchronously run under `runTest`; the 10A `OpenVerifyInquiryActivityComponentsTest` needed a looper-idling wait (applied in Stage 3).
6. Commit hygiene: its first stub commit carried foundation-10b's then-uncommitted `signals/Integrity.kt` unchanged; every later commit names its files.

### api-integrity

1. **C211.3 decided:** the session member is left out where no provider is bound, else `{request}` (true only for a created or pending, unredacted inquiry under the session-integrity tier with no token taken and no verdict); the challenge route answers 409 `integrity_challenge_invalid` whenever the session does not ask; the token post answers 202 with body `null`; the request hash is SHA-256 over `openverify/play-integrity/v1\n` + session id + `\n` + 16 random bytes, base64url.
2. **C211.4/C211.5 mapping:** a failed package, request-hash or timestamp check, or a failed decode, gives `integrity_decode_failed` on both signals; `app_recognized` is true only for `PLAY_RECOGNIZED` with a configured certificate digest; the level is the strongest of strong, device, basic, else `none` (`MEETS_VIRTUAL_INTEGRITY` kept, read as `none`); a taken token not yet decoded reads `collector_missing`; with a provider and no token, `integrity_token_unavailable` (an Android session collected), `not_android` (only web), else the sessions' gap; access tokens cached until 60 s before expiry.
3. **C210.4:** the independent families are where a probe reads (build properties, files and mounts, the process), so `device_hooked` is at most 0.5; sessions combine by Phase 7's rule; a code outside a signal's list is dropped; a `true` value's reasons are the codes only.
4. **C210.5:** on flows the dependent edges are the signal's route, the later routes, `else` and its `onNull` route; a path is held by a verification step followed by a branch route on that verification's status, attempts or attempts-remaining; the coupled limit is implemented for flows; on workflows the gate is a `run_verification` step followed by a conditional on its `steps.<id>.output(.object).status`, and `signal_not_decisive_alone` is an error in every environment.
5. **Repository widening:** `NewInquiryIntegrityChallenge.createdAt`; `readInquiryIntegrityToken` (a retryable decode keeps the token); `inquiryIntegrityTokenTaken`; the not-built errors removed. Borrowed edits under foundation-10b amendment 8: `config.ts` (three variables, the secret), `providers/index.ts`, `worker.ts`, `.env.example`; core's `bot_score` v3 and `automation_artefact_count` v2 with their pins.
6. **Hooks it left** (settled in Stage 3): the signal jobs' provider facts; `deviceIntegrityBound` at the two publish-report callers; 501 dropped from the routes' problems with the OpenAPI regenerated. Carried: a sweep for tokens whose decode job was never enqueued; the coupled-outcome limit on workflows for a trigger verification.

## Workflow 10B Stage 3 amendments (integration-10b)

Recorded by `integration-10b` on 2026-11-09 (from `76bcb109`; evidence in `docs/tdd/phase10-integration-10b.tdd.md`, each fix RED then GREEN). Binding on 10C onward.

1. **The activity components test (every Stage 1b and 2 blocker).** `OpenVerifyInquiryActivityComponentsTest.signalsBody()` waits in 50 ms steps and idles the paused main looper between them, since the probes read on `Dispatchers.IO` (C210.3). Any Robolectric activity test that waits for a request sent after an off-main read uses the same recipe.
2. **C211.3, the SDK sends.** `FlowApiClient.createIntegrityChallenge()` and `sendIntegrityToken()` go through `IntegrityRequests` over the client's own HTTP client, API base and authorization; the no-argument stubs and their test are gone; the two integrity request fixtures are written through the real client (byte-identical).
3. **C204, C206.1, C202.1, the coordinator and screen hooks.** `ActiveCapture.window()` carries `created.attributes.guidance`; `resolve`'s camera check and `uploadAllowed` read `nativeCaptureMethods(step)`; `captureGuideAspect(state)` draws `DocumentGuide.aspectOf(state)` on an ID side (the TD3 page on a passport-only front).
4. **C204.6, the launch's guidance.** `InquiryLaunchScope.captureGuidance = SelfieGuidanceFactory(components.faceDetectorFactory, { selfieWindow?.value?.guidance }, next = BuiltInCaptureGuidance)`, one per launch; the activity sets `scope.selfieWindow = viewModel.selfieCapture`, calls `SelfieExtensions.reset()` on a new launch (`savedInstanceState == null`) and provides `LocalCaptureGuidance`. Without a detector a selfie keeps the manual shutter.
5. **C211.5, the signal jobs.** `InquirySignalsInputContext.deviceIntegrity?` and `InquirySignalsDeps.providers?`; `deviceIntegrityContext(providers, kind)` gives the kind's bound provider (`null`: none) or nothing without bindings; `processInquirySignals` and `processFlowSignalSnapshot` pass it; `worker.ts` passes `providers` to both processors. A sandbox verdict reaches the two signals of a running inquiry.
6. **C221.10.** Both integrity routes document no 501; `docs/api/openapi.json` regenerated once (sha256 `407571a0406cf2b1b0897be272388fcfe4a2f2f44470e0d8087062e5f7b3b0d4`). `@openverify/node` needs no regeneration (its resource types are `@openverify/contracts`'), and no `contracts/fixtures/api/*` file is generated from a 10B route schema.
7. **Decision 66, done here (contracts-10b's blocker 1).** `notice.play-integrity` joins `COPY_KEYS` (`noticeText()`, 21 keys) and the five web catalogs; `nativeApp.notice.session-integrity` names the probes' reads; the web's `notice.session-integrity` is unchanged. Re-exported in order: `i18n:export` (web rows under `web-a8a42feea15e8a73`, appended), `copy-keys.json`, the key map (27 entries; `openverify_notice_play_integrity`, flowKey = catalogKey, legal `notice`), `SDK_COPY_KEYS`, `ConsentTexts`, `FlowCoordinator.Settings.consentTextKeys`, `generateLocaleStrings`, `exportConsentTexts` (45 Android rows under `android-0.1.0-6ed3979d273c180e`, appended), the digests and the consent request fixtures. Non-English texts are machine-drafted (KP10-16).
8. **C200.4 amended (a defect the second Android version exposed).** `android-consent-digests.json` holds the latest Android catalog version's rows only (the last appended in `consent-texts.json`), since it checks the current SDK's texts; earlier rows stay in the table and the server keeps accepting their digests.
9. **C211.9 wired.** `publishRulesInTx` and `validateTemplateVersion` pass `deviceIntegrityBound: deps.providers[kind].deviceIntegrity !== undefined`, so `play_integrity_notice_missing` applies to v2 flows (v1 flows, the default template among them, are not analysed). Consequence for every v2 flow on an install that binds the provider: its consent shows `notice.play-integrity` beside the session-integrity notice.
10. **The stack and compose.** `infra/docker-compose.yml` passes the `deviceIntegrity` binding to the API and the worker alike and the three Play Integrity settings to the worker only (they reached no container before). The e2e stack binds `deviceIntegrity` to `sandbox` for sandbox environments, never `play-integrity`, and passes no Play Integrity setting; therefore `e2e/tests/support/flow-v2.ts`'s `consentStep()` and the 10A Android fixture show `notice.play-integrity`.
11. **C224.3, the 10B spec.** `e2e/tests/device-integrity.spec.ts` (two tests, chromium shard 1): an API-driven client with the Android `User-Agent` and `OpenVerify-Flow-Spec: 2`; one Android signals body; one challenge and one sandbox magic token, the decode awaited through the verdict row's presence; then the frozen signals: emulator and hooking `true` with reasons, rooted `null` (`no_indicator_found`), the debugger evidence in `bot_score` and `automation_artefact_count` 1, `behavior_threat_level` `null` (`input_missing`), `strong`/`true` from `sandbox-strong`; a clean body reads every indicator `null`, `bot_score` `no_artefact_found`, the count 0 and `device`/`false` from `sandbox-unrecognized-app`; nothing declined.
12. **CI.** The SDK host-test floor is 2,321 (1,934 + 26 + 352 + 10 - 1); the emulator step prints `adb logcat -d -s openverify-probes OpenVerifyCameraTest` after a passing run, so the probes' total time and the barcode copy costs are in the job log; the new device tests run through the existing `connectedAndroidDeviceTest`. Job times at the 10A push (run 42603210118): unit-api 20.9 of 40, unit-dashboard 10.8 of 20, node 13.8 of 25, unit-db 12.7 of 25, integration 14.9 of 30, lint 9.6 of 30, vision 9.2 of 30, e2e 40.6 of 80 (full stack well under 75% of the 60-minute `globalTimeout`); none within 25% of its timeout, so no split. The android job's runner time is unknown (it failed at configuration, KP10-4); estimated 9.3 (Phase 9) plus 3-5 for the screenshot step plus about 20% more host tests: within 45.
13. **Docs.** `docs/android/capture.md` and `docs/android/device-integrity.md` (with the host-side `IntegrityTokenProvider` recipe: the standard request prepared lazily on the first `token()` call, the 10,000-a-day quota, sideloaded and self-built apps unrecognized), `docs/checks/signals.md` (the five signals, `integrity_unevaluated`'s adverse cause, the debugger facts, the publish rules and their limit), `docs/checks/selfie.md` (on-device guidance on Android, C204.8), `docs/checks/sandbox-providers.md` (the magic tokens), `docs/compliance/session-integrity.md` (the strictly-necessary template with the probe list), the SDK README's Data safety row for `capture-method` and `capture-trigger`.
14. **Carried** (with reasons in the PROGRESS draft): KP10-4 until the push's CI; one constant for the 1280 px barcode width; a session member naming the barcode-bearing side; `extendCapture` returning its outcome instead of `SelfieExtensions`; `client-elapsed-ms` past ten extensions; a sweep for tokens whose decode was never enqueued; the coupled limit for a trigger verification on workflows; `StateHooksTest`'s device-integrity pin still asserts the stub-era "requests nothing" over a session without `integrity` (still true, now covered by `IntegrityCoordinatorTest`).

## Orchestrator decisions before Workflow 10C (2026-11-10)

Taken by the orchestrator under the owner's delegation of 2026-11-08 ("Make the other open decisions yourself and record them in the plan"), from the re-check of `workflow-10c.mjs` against Workflow 10B as built at `f71b0f6d`. Binding over the plan's earlier text; later sections bind over this one. From this Workflow on the orchestrator and its agents run on `claude-fable-5-2` (10A and 10B ran on `claude-opus-5-5`).

1. **Base and start.** 10C's base is `f71b0f6d` (10B's PROGRESS entry and push); this section's commit is its `start`. The script takes `{base, start, prior, known}` as 10B did. `start` is not pushed alone.
2. **The 10B push's CI** (run 42762952128 on `f71b0f6d`, read 2026-11-10): 17 jobs green. `android` took 21 of 45 minutes with the screenshot comparison on the GitHub runner (KP10-4 closed); `secrets` verified its cached gitleaks copy (KP10-2 proved); e2e took 39 of 80. `android-instrumented` ran on the emulator (API 34, `google_apis`, x86_64, 2 cores, a 320x640 display) for the first time since 42476248527: 7 of 9 device tests passed and 2 failed, KP10-21 and KP10-22.
3. **No CI run to count on** (owner, 2026-11-10: "I am out of GitHub minutes so do most of testing locally"). This amends C219.7, the Rules' "Device tests" and "CI cadence" lines and Validation's "run only in CI" comments for 10C onward, until the owner says minutes are back.
   - A device-only behaviour is proved in three parts: (a) a host test of its logic, RED then GREEN; (b) the device test kept or extended, never weakened, compiled and lint-checked locally; (c) a followups line "device proof open: <test>: <what it would prove>", which the integration agent carries into PROGRESS.
   - Nobody dispatches, re-runs or pushes for a check. A missing or never-started run is not a failure of the code and is not waited for.
   - `ci.yml` and `android-release.yml` are still written, and held by `infra/tests/ci-workflow.test.sh`.
   - The orchestrator still pushes once per Workflow after a green full local validation (a fast-forward to the private repository costs no minutes); it records whether a run started.
4. **Decision 72 and Acceptance's "done means CI-green".** The bar stays and waits on Actions minutes. Until a run exists, PROGRESS says "done locally; CI pending" and lists what is unproved on a device. C220.3's release-matrix dispatch after 10D's push waits on the same.
5. **carry-10c.** The plan's 10C section says "Eight agents"; 10C has nine. carry-10c runs in Stage 1a beside contracts-10c (as carry-10a ran in 10A) and is awaited before Stage 2, so no Stage 2 agent meets its RED commits.

   - contracts-10c "runs alone" now means alone but for carry-10c, whose paths it does not edit. If carry-10c commits nothing, its items pass to integration-10c.

F11's copy-cost measurement did not print.
